Single-thread download.\\\")\n    r = requests.get(URL, stream=True, timeout=300)\n    with open(OUTPUT, 'wb') as f:\n        dl = 0\n        t0 = time.time()\n        for chunk in r.iter_content(chunk_size=8*1024*1024):\n            f.write(chunk)\n            dl += len(chunk)\n            elapsed = time.time() - t0\n            speed = dl / elapsed / 1024**2\n            pct = dl / total_size * 100\n            print(f\\\"\\\\r{pct:.1f}% | {speed:.1f} MB/s\\\", end='', flush=True)\n    print(f\\\"\\\\nDone: {os.path.getsize(OUTPUT):,} bytes\\\")\n    sys.exit(0)\n\n# Pre-allocate\nwith open(OUTPUT, 'wb') as f:\n    f.truncate(total_size)\n\nchunks = []\nfor start in range(0, total_size, CHUNK_SIZE):\n    end = min(start + CHUNK_SIZE - 1, total_size - 1)\n    chunks.append((start, end))\nprint(f\\\"Chunks: {len(chunks)} x {CHUNK_SIZE/1024**2:.0f}MB, Threads: {NUM_THREADS}\\\")\n\ndownloaded = [0]\nlock = threading.Lock()\nt0 = time.time()\n\ndef dl_chunk(idx, start, end):\n    for retry in range(10):\n        try:\n            sess = requests.Session()\n            r = sess.get(URL, headers={'Range': f'bytes={start}-{end}'}, \n                        timeout=300, stream=True)\n            if r.status_code not in (200, 206):\n                raise Exception(f\\\"HTTP {r.status_code}\\\")\n            data = bytearray()\n            for chunk in r.iter_content(chunk_size=1024*1024):\n                data.extend(chunk)\n            with open(OUTPUT, 'r+b') as f:\n                f.seek(start)\n                f.write(data)\n            with lock:\n                downloaded[0] += len(data)\n            return len(data)\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\\\"Chunk {idx} retry {retry}: {e}\\\")\n            time.sleep(5 * (retry + 1))\n    return 0\n\nwith ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=NUM_THREADS) as pool:\n    futures = {pool.submit(dl_chunk, i, s, e): i for i, (s, e) in enumerate(chunks)}\n    done = 0\n    for f in as_completed(futures):\n        done += 1\n        elapsed = time.time() - t0\n        speed = downloaded[0] / elapsed / 1024**2 if elapsed > 0 else 0\n        pct = downloaded[0] / total_size * 100\n        eta_min = (total_size - downloaded[0]) / max(downloaded[0] / elapsed, 1) / 60\n        print(f\\\"[{done}/{len(chunks)}] {pct:.1f}% | {speed:.1f} MB/s | ETA {eta_min:.0f}min\\\")\n\nelapsed = time.time() - t0\nprint(f\\\"\\\\nDone in {elapsed/60:.1f}min ({total_size/elapsed/1024**2:.1f} MB/s)\\\")\n\nfrom safetensors import safe_open\nsf = safe_open(OUTPUT, framework='pt')\nfor key in ['projector.fc1.weight', 'projector.fc2.weight', 'projector.fc3.weight']:\n    t = sf.get_tensor(key)\n    print(f\\\"{key}: norm={t.float().norm().item():.4f}\\\")\nprint(\\\"DOWNLOAD_VERIFIED\\\")\nPYEOF\nnohup python3 /tmp/parallel_download2.py > /tmp/redownload_shard1.log 2>&1 & echo PID=\\$!\""

{
  "file_path": "/tmp/test_forward.py",
  "content": "import torch\nimport h5py\nfrom PIL import Image\nfrom transformers import AutoModelForVision2Seq, AutoProcessor\n\nproc = AutoProcessor.from_pretrained('/root/autodl-tmp/vla-ssd/checkpoints/openvla-7b', trust_remote_code=True)\nmodel = AutoModelForVision2Seq.from_pretrained(\n    '/root/autodl-tmp/vla-ssd/checkpoints/openvla-7b',\n    torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16, trust_remote_code=True, low_cpu_mem_usage=True,\n    attn_implementation='eager'\n).to('cuda:0').eval()\n\nf = h5py.File('/root/autodl-tmp/vla-ssd/data/libero/libero_10/KITCHEN_SCENE3_turn_on_the_stove_and_put_the_moka_pot_on_it_demo.hdf5', 'r')\nimg0 = Image.fromarray(f['data']['demo_0']['obs']['agentview_rgb'][0])\nimg100 = Image.fromarray(f['data']['demo_0']['obs']['agentview_rgb'][100])\nf.close()\n\nprompt = 'In: What action should the robot take to turn on the stove and put the moka pot on it?\\nOut:'\n\nwith torch.no_grad():\n    inp0 = proc(text=prompt, images=img0, return_tensors='pt')\n    inp0 = {k: v.to('cuda:0') for k, v in inp0.items()}\n    out0 = model(**inp0, output_hidden_states=True)\n\n    inp100 = proc(text=prompt, images=img100, return_tensors='pt')\n    inp100 = {k: v.to('cuda:0') for k, v in inp100.items()}\n    out100 = model(**inp100, output_hidden_states=True)\n\nfor li in [0, 16, 32]:\n    h0 = out0.hidden_states[li][0]\n    h1 = out100.hidden_states[li][0]\n    lp_diff = (h0[-1].float() - h1[-1].float()).abs().max().item()\n    im_diff = (h0[1:257].float().mean(0) - h1[1:257].float().mean(0)).abs().max().item()\n    print(f'Layer {li:2d}: lp_diff={lp_diff:.6f}, im_diff={im_diff:.6f}')\n\ndiff32 = (out0.hidden_states[32][0][-1].float() - out100.hidden_states[32][0][-1].float()).abs().max().item()\nassert diff32 > 0.001, f'Features still identical at layer 32! diff={diff32}'\nprint('FORWARD_TEST_PASSED')\n"
}

Check if wget is still running\n    if ! pgrep -f 'wget.*model-00001' > /dev/null 2>&1; then\n        if [ \\\"$SIZE\\\" -ge \\\"$EXPECTED_SIZE\\\" ]; then\n            echo \\\"Download complete!\\\"\n            break\n        else\n            echo \\\"ERROR: wget exited but file incomplete ($SIZE < $EXPECTED_SIZE)\\\"\n            exit 1\n        fi\n    fi\n    sleep 60\ndone\n\necho \\\"\\\"\necho \\\"=== Step 1: Verify shard integrity ===\\\"\npython3 /root/vla-ssd/scripts/verify_shard.py\nif [ $? -ne 0 ]; then\n    echo \\\"SHARD VERIFICATION FAILED\\\"\n    exit 1\nfi\n\necho \\\"\\\"\necho \\\"=== Step 2: Clear HF cache (use patched code from checkpoint) ===\\\"\nrm -rf /root/.cache/huggingface/modules/transformers_modules/openvla_hyphen_7b/\n\necho \\\"\\\"\necho \\\"=== Step 3: Dry-run feature extraction ===\\\"\ncd /root/vla-ssd\nrm -rf /tmp/dryrun_features/\nHDF5_USE_FILE_LOCKING=FALSE CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 python scripts/extract_features.py \\\n    --model_path /root/autodl-tmp/vla-ssd/checkpoints/openvla-7b \\\n    --data_dir /root/autodl-tmp/vla-ssd/data/libero/libero_10/ \\\n    --output_dir /tmp/dryrun_features/ \\\n    --gpu 0 --max_demos 1\n\necho \\\"\\\"\necho \\\"=== Step 4: Validate dry-run output ===\\\"\npython3 -c \"\nimport h5py, numpy as np, glob\nfiles = sorted(glob.glob('/tmp/dryrun_features/*.h5'))\nprint(f'Output files: {len(files)}')\nfor fp in files[:2]:\n    f = h5py.File(fp, 'r')\n    dk = list(f.keys())[0]\n    lp = f[dk]['last_preaction'][:].astype(np.float32)\n    im = f[dk]['image_mean'][:].astype(np.float32)\n    # Check temporal variation\n    t_var_lp = np.abs(lp[0,32,:] - lp[-1,32,:]).max()\n    t_var_im = np.abs(im[0,32,:] - im[-1,32,:]).max()\n    print(f'{fp.split(\\\"/\\\")[-1]}: shape={lp.shape}, temporal_var_lp={t_var_lp:.4f}, temporal_var_im={t_var_im:.4f}')\n    assert t_var_lp > 0.001, 'Last_preaction still identical across time!'\n    f.close()\nprint('DRYRUN_VALIDATION_PASSED')\n\"\n\necho \\\"\\\"\necho \\\"=== Step 5: Generate untrained model ===\\\"\nCUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1 python scripts/init_untrained.py \\\n    --model_path /root/autodl-tmp/vla-ssd/checkpoints/openvla-7b \\\n    --output_path /root/autodl-tmp/vla-ssd/checkpoints/openvla-7b-untrained/\n\necho \\\"\\\"\necho \\\"=== Step 6: Verify untrained model projector ===\\\"\npython3 -c \"\nfrom safetensors import safe_open\nimport glob\nfiles = glob.glob('/root/autodl-tmp/vla-ssd/checkpoints/openvla-7b-untrained/*.safetensors')\nfor f in sorted(files):\n    sf = safe_open(f, framework='pt')\n    for key in sf.keys():\n        if 'projector.fc' in key:\n            t = sf.get_tensor(key)\n            norm = t.float().norm().item()\n            print(f'{key}: norm={norm:.4f}')\n            if 'fc2' in key or 'fc3' in key:\n                assert norm > 0.01, f'{key} is zero!'\nprint('UNTRAINED_MODEL_VERIFIED')\n\"\n\necho \\\"\\\"\necho \\\"[$(date)] === ALL STEPS COMPLETE ===\\\"\necho \\\"Ready to submit full extraction jobs.\\\"\nSHEOF\nchmod +x /root/vla-ssd/scripts/post_download_pipeline.sh\necho 'Pipeline script created'\""

Wait, the pipeline script checks for `pgrep -f 'wget.*model-00001'` but we're using aria2c now, not wget. The pipeline won't correctly detect when the download completes. I need to fix this.

Also, the pipeline checks `sz >= EXPECTED_SIZE` but with aria2c creating a sparse file, `os.path.getsize()` will return the apparent file size (6.5 GB) immediately, not the actual downloaded size. So the pipeline might think the download is already complete.

Let me kill the pipeline and fix it. I need to check for `aria2c` process instead of `wget`, and use `os.stat(SHARD).st_blocks * 512` instead of `os.path.getsize()` to get actual data size.


Actually, there's a simpler approach—aria2c creates a `.aria2` control file during the download and removes it when finished, so I can just monitor for that file's absence instead.
```

1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	import os, sys, time, subprocess, glob
3	import numpy as np
4	
5	SHARD = '/root/autodl-tmp/vla-ssd/checkpoints/openvla-7b/model-00001-of-00003.safetensors'
6	EXPECTED_SIZE = 6948961960
7	
8	def run(cmd):
9	    print(f'Running: {cmd}')
10	    r = subprocess.run(cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True)
11	    print(r.stdout)
12	    if r.stderr:
13	        print(r.stderr, file=sys.stderr)
14	    if r.returncode != 0:
15	        print(f'FAILED with exit code {r.returncode}')
16	        sys.exit(1)
17	    return r.stdout
18	
19	print(f'[{time.strftime("%H:%M:%S")}] Waiting for download...')
20	while True:
21	    if not os.path.exists(SHARD):
22	        time.sleep(30)
23	        continue
24	    sz = os.path.getsize(SHARD)
25	    pct = sz * 100 // EXPECTED_SIZE
26	    print(f'  {sz/1024**3:.2f} GB / {EXPECTED_SIZE/1024**3:.2f} GB ({pct}%)')
27	    if sz >= EXPECTED_SIZE:
28	        r = subprocess.run(['pgrep', '-f', 'wget.*model-00001'], capture_output=True)
29	        if r.returncode != 0:
30	            print('Download complete!')
31	            break
32	    time.sleep(60)
33	
34	print('\n=== Step 1: Verify shard ===')
35	from safetensors import safe_open
36	sf = safe_open(SHARD, framework='pt')
37	for key in ['projector.fc2.weight', 'projector.fc3.weight']:
38	    t = sf.get_tensor(key)
39	    norm = t.float().norm().item()
40	    print(f'{key}: norm={norm:.4f}')
41	    assert norm > 0.01, f'{key} is ZERO!'
42	print('VERIFIED')
43	
44	print('\n=== Step 2: Clear HF cache ===')
45	os.system('rm -rf /root/.cache/huggingface/modules/transformers_modules/openvla_hyphen_7b/')
46	
47	print('\n=== Step 3: Dry-run ===')
48	os.system('rm -rf /tmp/dryrun_features/')
49	run('cd /root/vla-ssd && HDF5_USE_FILE_LOCKING=FALSE CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 python scripts/extract_features.py --model_path /root/autodl-tmp/vla-ssd/checkpoints/openvla-7b --data_dir /root/autodl-tmp/vla-ssd/data/libero/libero_10/ --output_dir /tmp/dryrun_features/ --gpu 0 --max_demos 1')
50	
51	print('\n=== Step 4: Validate ===')
52	import h5py
53	files = sorted(glob.glob('/tmp/dryrun_features/*.h5'))
54	print(f'Files: {len(files)}')
55	for fp in files[:2]:
56	    f = h5py.File(fp, 'r')
57	    dk = list(f.keys())[0]
58	    lp = f[dk]['last_preaction'][:].astype(np.float32)
59	    tvar = np.abs(lp[0,32,:] - lp[-1,32,:]).max()
60	    print(f'{os.path.basename(fp)}: shape={lp.shape}, temporal_var={tvar:.4f}')
61	    assert tvar > 0.001, 'Still identical!'
62	    f.close()
63	print('DRYRUN_VALIDATED')
64	
65	print('\n=== Step 5: Init untrained ===')
66	run('cd /root/vla-ssd && CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1 python scripts/init_untrained.py --model_path /root/autodl-tmp/vla-ssd/checkpoints/openvla-7b --output_path /root/autodl-tmp/vla-ssd/checkpoints/openvla-7b-untrained/')
67	
68	print(f'\n[{time.strftime("%H:%M:%S")}] === ALL STEPS COMPLETE ===')
69

This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user requested a complete OpenVLA 7B feature extraction pipeline on AutoDL server westd-19183 (2×RTX PRO 6000 GPUs):
   - **Step 1**: Dry-run with `--max_demos 1` to validate script correctness, processor output structure, hidden_states, token positions, and HDF5 compatibility
   - **Step 2**: Initialize untrained model (reinitialize LLM backbone, keep vision encoder + projector)
   - **Step 3**: Submit trained model full extraction on cuda:0 (50 demos × 10 tasks) via `submit_training_job`
   - **Step 4**: Submit untrained model full extraction on cuda:1 (50 demos × 10 tasks) via `submit_training_job`
   
   The user provided specific commands, paths, verification requirements, and error handling guidance. Expected ~10-12 hours per extraction job.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - OpenVLA 7B: Vision-Language-Action model based on Prismatic architecture (DINOv2 + SigLIP fused vision backbone, LLaMA LLM)
   - Token layout: [BOS] [256 image tokens] [text tokens...]
   - Feature extraction: `last_preaction` (h[-1], last sequence position) and `image_mean` (h[1:257].mean(0), mean of 256 image tokens)
   - 33 hidden layers (embedding + 32 transformer layers), 4096 hidden dim
   - HDF5 output format with gzip compression, float16
   - Prismatic projector: fused version with fc1(2176→8704) → GELU → fc2(8704→4096) → GELU → fc3(4096→4096)
   - LIBERO benchmark: 10 tasks, 50 demos each, ~250 timesteps per demo
   - transformers 4.57.6 (incompatible with OpenVLA's expected 4.40.1)
   - timm 1.0.27 (incompatible with OpenVLA's expected 0.9.x)
   - AutoDL environment: conda base, network_turbo proxy, data disk at /root/autodl-tmp/

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - **`/root/vla-ssd/scripts/extract_features.py`** (remote)
     - Main feature extraction script. Loads OpenVLA, processes LIBERO HDF5 demos, extracts hidden states per timestep
     - Modified: Added `attn_implementation="eager"` to `from_pretrained` call (line 84)
     - Key function `extract_one_task()`: batches images, runs forward pass with `output_hidden_states=True`, extracts `h[-1]` and `h[1:1+256].mean(0)` per layer
     - Key function `load_model()`: loads model with trust_remote_code, optionally reinitializes LLM backbone
     ```python
     model = AutoModelForVision2Seq.from_pretrained(
         model_path,
         torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16,
         trust_remote_code=True,
         low_cpu_mem_usage=True,
         attn_implementation="eager",
     )
